# Unit 21 linguistic and owned-edition mapping packet

This packet prepares review. It records an unsigned structural mapping proposal. It does not record a qualified review, an approved edition alignment, or an install decision.

The owned-edition mapping worksheet separates observed edition facts, the proposed lesson and printed page range, unresolved edition questions, and matters that still require qualified linguistic review. ISBN, owned-copy verification, variance, reviewer identity, qualification, date, and approval stay blank. The disposition remains PENDING_CURRICULUM_REVIEWER.

Unit 21 remains in development. Its readiness is content-complete and review-pending. The Lesson 21 relationship is a proposed, unsigned mapping. It is not an approved edition alignment.

title=Units 21–25 source notice
summary=Proposed Units 21–25 adapt admitted CC BY-SA 4.0 spans. Pressbooks evidence is separate from Wikimedia evidence. Bounded completion practice is review-pending, not full coverage.
detail=Wikibooks excerpts are frozen by oldid under the CC BY-SA 4.0 route recorded on Wikibooks:Copyrights oldid 4622060. The adapted selection and arrangement are share-alike. Application code, user data, and user recordings are outside that statement. No audio is included. Linked audio on the useful-words page is not admitted. Dated name-page claims about social address, foreigners, naming creativity, and the number of names are not shipped. English Wikipedia Russian grammar oldid 1369617627 is used under CC BY-SA 4.0 via Wikipedia:Copyrights oldid 1369466282. Russian Wikipedia pages are used under CC BY-SA 4.0 via the Russian Wikipedia copyrights page oldid 154562152. The one-and-a-half entry is frozen at English Wiktionary oldid 89737719 under CC BY-SA 4.0 via Wiktionary:Copyrights oldid 87545419. Linked audio is not admitted. Unit 23 time expressions are frozen Pressbooks chapter bytes sha256 87815b8724cb11aecba5bb6d8b6438f783408f2354e2560a3c0fe026fac1e171, retrieved 2026-09-26T22:52:41Z, under that chapter's own CC BY-SA 4.0 footer. That Pressbooks route is not a Wikipedia, Wiktionary, or Wikibooks license. Russian Wikipedia personal-name page oldid 154915954 is retained for bounded classification of the documented full-form and short-form diminutive bases, the dismissive-suffix limitation, and the lowered-status note, under the Russian Wikipedia copyrights page oldid 154562152. A separate application chain on that same span uses only names already in the span. It is not a name generator. Completion over a period is not prepared. The Unit 24 word-order pair is bounded sentence recognition on English Wikipedia Russian grammar oldid 1369617627. It does not clear scope-sensitive placement. Scope-sensitive placement is the ReadyRussian Language Generator 3 statement that the modifier negates the following word, and that page's two-sentence contrast, under the ReadyRussian CC BY-SA 4.0 route. Review is pending. Bounded practice for completion over a period is implemented and review-pending. It is not approved coverage of every temporal clause. Book work stays in the copy you own.

recordType=meaning
sourceUrl=https://en.wikibooks.org/wiki/Russian/Possessive_Pronouns?oldid=4220315
sourceRevision=4220315
sourceRecordId=possessive:The reflexive pronouns (свой his/her/its own m., своя his/her/its own f., своё h
originalSourceText=The reflexive pronouns (свой his/her/its own m., своя his/her/its own f., своё his/her/its own n., свои his/her/its own pl.), as well as all possessive nouns except the 3rd person ones take the gender and the quantity of the object in possession:

:"фильм" (movie) is of masculine gender, and:
::Бо́ря рассказа́л о своём люби́мом фи́льме.  (Borya told about his favorite movie.)
::А́нна рассказа́ла о своём люби́мом фи́льме.  (Anna told about her favorite movie.)

::"Я не ви́дел твоего́ люби́мого фи́льма, Бо́ря."  (I didn't see your favorite movie, Borya).
::"Я не ви́дел твоего́ люби́мого фи́льма, А́нна." (I didn't see your favorite movie, Anna.)

:"кни́га" (book) is of feminine gender, and:
::Свое́й кни́ги он никогда́ не написа́л.  (He never wrote his own book.)
::Свое́й кни́ги она́ никогда́ не написа́ла.  (She never wrote her own book.)

::"Мо́жно, возьму́ твою́ кни́гу, Бо́ря?"  (Can I take your book, Borya?)
::"Мо́жно, возьму́ твою́ кни́гу, А́нна?"  (Can I take your book, Anna?)

:"джи́нсы" (jeans) is a plural-only noun, and:
::Бо́ря наде́л свои́ джи́нсы.  (Borya put on his own jeans.)
::А́нна наде́ла свои́ джи́нсы.  (Anna put on her own jeans.)
shippedText=(Borya told about his favorite movie.)
transformation=selection
licenseId=CC BY-SA 4.0
licenseUrl=https://creativecommons.org/licenses/by-sa/4.0/
attribution=Wikibooks contributors, CC BY-SA 4.0. Page history for each frozen oldid identifies the contributors.
shareAlikeTreatment=Adapted curriculum data, selection, and arrangement are distributed under CC BY-SA 4.0. Application code, user data, and user recordings are outside that statement. No audio is relicensed because this unit ships no audio.
sourceHash=0787c9ab956e064de03a3daba2e9b79646510006161e2001fe844f97da50ef42

sourceRecordId=possessive:The reflexive pronouns (свой his/her/its own m., своя his/her/its own f., своё h
originalSourceText=The reflexive pronouns (свой his/her/its own m., своя his/her/its own f., своё his/her/its own n., свои his/her/its own pl.), as well as all possessive nouns except the 3rd person ones take the gender and the quantity of the object in possession:

:"фильм" (movie) is of masculine gender, and:
::Бо́ря рассказа́л о своём люби́мом фи́льме.  (Borya told about his favorite movie.)
::А́нна рассказа́ла о своём люби́мом фи́льме.  (Anna told about her favorite movie.)

::"Я не ви́дел твоего́ люби́мого фи́льма, Бо́ря."  (I didn't see your favorite movie, Borya).
::"Я не ви́дел твоего́ люби́мого фи́льма, А́нна." (I didn't see your favorite movie, Anna.)

:"кни́га" (book) is of feminine gender, and:
::Свое́й кни́ги он никогда́ не написа́л.  (He never wrote his own book.)
::Свое́й кни́ги она́ никогда́ не написа́ла.  (She never wrote her own book.)

::"Мо́жно, возьму́ твою́ кни́гу, Бо́ря?"  (Can I take your book, Borya?)
::"Мо́жно, возьму́ твою́ кни́гу, А́нна?"  (Can I take your book, Anna?)

:"джи́нсы" (jeans) is a plural-only noun, and:
::Бо́ря наде́л свои́ джи́нсы.  (Borya put on his own jeans.)
::А́нна наде́ла свои́ джи́нсы.  (Anna put on her own jeans.)
shippedText=(Anna told about her favorite movie.)

::"Я не ви́дел твоего́ люби́мого фи́льма, А́нна." (I didn't see your favorite movie, Anna.)

:"кни́га" (book) is of feminine gender, and:
::Свое́й кни́ги он никогда́ не написа́л.  (He never wrote his own book.)
::Свое́й кни́ги она́ никогда́ не написа́ла.  (She never wrote her own book.)

::"Мо́жно, возьму́ твою́ кни́гу, Бо́ря?"  (Can I take your book, Borya?)
::"Мо́жно, возьму́ твою́ кни́гу, А́нна?"  (Can I take your book, Anna?)

:"джи́нсы" (jeans) is a plural-only noun, and:
::Бо́ря наде́л свои́ джи́нсы.  (Borya put on his own jeans.)
::А́нна наде́ла свои́ джи́нсы.  (Anna put on her own jeans.)

recordType=instruction
sourceUrl=https://en.wikibooks.org/wiki/Russian/Possessive_Pronouns?oldid=4220315
sourceRevision=4220315
sourceRecordId=possessive:The reflexive pronouns (свой his/her/its own m., своя his/her/its own f., своё h
originalSourceText=The reflexive pronouns (свой his/her/its own m., своя his/her/its own f., своё his/her/its own n., свои his/her/its own pl.), as well as all possessive nouns except the 3rd person ones take the gender and the quantity of the object in possession:

:"фильм" (movie) is of masculine gender, and:
::Бо́ря рассказа́л о своём люби́мом фи́льме.  (Borya told about his favorite movie.)
::А́нна рассказа́ла о своём люби́мом фи́льме.  (Anna told about her favorite movie.)

::"Я не ви́дел твоего́ люби́мого фи́льма, Бо́ря."  (I didn't see your favorite movie, Borya).
::"Я не ви́дел твоего́ люби́мого фи́льма, А́нна." (I didn't see your favorite movie, Anna.)

:"кни́га" (book) is of feminine gender, and:
::Свое́й кни́ги он никогда́ не написа́л.  (He never wrote his own book.)
::Свое́й кни́ги она́ никогда́ не написа́ла.  (She never wrote her own book.)

::"Мо́жно, возьму́ твою́ кни́гу, Бо́ря?"  (Can I take your book, Borya?)
::"Мо́жно, возьму́ твою́ кни́гу, А́нна?"  (Can I take your book, Anna?)

:"джи́нсы" (jeans) is a plural-only noun, and:
::Бо́ря наде́л свои́ джи́нсы.  (Borya put on his own jeans.)
::А́нна наде́ла свои́ джи́нсы.  (Anna put on her own jeans.)
shippedText=The reflexive pronouns (свой his/her/its own m., своя his/her/its own f., своё his/her/its own n., свои his/her/its own pl.), as well as all possessive nouns except the 3rd person ones take the gender and the quantity of the object in possession:

recordType=example
sourceUrl=https://en.wikibooks.org/wiki/Russian/Possessive_Pronouns?oldid=4220315
sourceRevision=4220315
sourceRecordId=possessive:The reflexive pronouns (свой his/her/its own m., своя his/her/its own f., своё h
originalSourceText=The reflexive pronouns (свой his/her/its own m., своя his/her/its own f., своё his/her/its own n., свои his/her/its own pl.), as well as all possessive nouns except the 3rd person ones take the gender and the quantity of the object in possession:

:"фильм" (movie) is of masculine gender, and:
::Бо́ря рассказа́л о своём люби́мом фи́льме.  (Borya told about his favorite movie.)
::А́нна рассказа́ла о своём люби́мом фи́льме.  (Anna told about her favorite movie.)

::"Я не ви́дел твоего́ люби́мого фи́льма, Бо́ря."  (I didn't see your favorite movie, Borya).
::"Я не ви́дел твоего́ люби́мого фи́льма, А́нна." (I didn't see your favorite movie, Anna.)

:"кни́га" (book) is of feminine gender, and:
::Свое́й кни́ги он никогда́ не написа́л.  (He never wrote his own book.)
::Свое́й кни́ги она́ никогда́ не написа́ла.  (She never wrote her own book.)

::"Мо́жно, возьму́ твою́ кни́гу, Бо́ря?"  (Can I take your book, Borya?)
::"Мо́жно, возьму́ твою́ кни́гу, А́нна?"  (Can I take your book, Anna?)

:"джи́нсы" (jeans) is a plural-only noun, and:
::Бо́ря наде́л свои́ джи́нсы.  (Borya put on his own jeans.)
::А́нна наде́ла свои́ джи́нсы.  (Anna put on her own jeans.)
